<!-- ORIGINAL T2-9 SPEC START — pruned 2026-05-18 — kept commented for one cycle of audit traceability
- **id:** T2-9
- **title:** `fw-save`: bincode-based save format + version-migration enum chain — first schema version locked at `1`
- **started:** 2026-05-18
- **task class:** architecture-cross-crate (save-schema lock; canonical-state-adjacent; foundational + irreversible — V1 schema decisions ship forever)
- **required subagent:** none (main thread — tight scope ~200 LoC; clear contract; bin-crate-equivalent — pure Rust types + tests; no canonical-hash-pin path)
- **selection note:** T2-9 is the FINAL MVP row before T2-10 phase-gate Codex review. T2-7 still BLOCKED on T2-4 (BLOCKED on missing `design/player-generation.md`).

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

1. **`SaveEnvelope` enum carries TWO variants** — `V0(SaveV0)` (a fictional pre-T2-9 stub representing what a minimal-schema save would have looked like) + `V1(SaveV1)` (the locked first-real-schema; existing `career_seed + content_pack_version + ledger` shape preserved).
2. **V0 → V1 forward migration** — `migrate_v0_to_v1(v0: SaveV0) -> SaveV1` function preserves V0's `career_seed` exactly + supplies sensible defaults for the new V1 fields (`content_pack_version = 1`, `ledger = MemoryLedger::new()`).
3. **`load_envelope(bytes) -> Result<SaveV1, SaveError>` migrates transparently** — decodes the envelope; if V0, runs migration; if V1, returns directly; if unknown variant, returns `SaveError::UnsupportedVersion` (forward-incompat-failure path).
4. **Four-test discipline for V0 → V1** (per `design/specs/save-migration-fixtures.md` references in `docs/specs/determinism-gate.md` + the file header):
   - **forward-migration** — V0 bytes load via `load_envelope` and emerge as the correct V1 payload with `career_seed` preserved + V1-only fields at their documented defaults.
   - **callback-preservation** — every V0 field maps deterministically to a V1 field (no silent drops); cross-checked by constructing V0 with a non-default `career_seed` + asserting the migrated V1's `career_seed == V0.career_seed`.
   - **forward-incompat-failure** — bytes claiming a future variant (`V99`) FAIL to load with a structured `SaveError::UnsupportedVersion { discriminant: 99 }` rather than silently default or panic. Construct the bytes manually since bincode's tag byte is the first byte of the envelope's discriminant.
   - **round-trip-byte-identical** — `encode(SaveEnvelope::V1(x))` → `decode(bytes)` → re-encode produces byte-identical bytes; existing test extended with this assertion.
5. **`SaveError` enum gains `UnsupportedVersion { discriminant: u32 }` variant** — used by the forward-incompat path; serialized to a clean `{ kind: "unsupportedVersion", discriminant: N }` shape if it ever crosses the wire (today it's purely in-crate).
6. **No canonical hash drift** — fw-save is not in the canonical-state pin path; the changes are schema-additive + new tests.

### AC-to-test matrix

| Acceptance criterion | Exact test or visual check | Observable that proves it |
|---|---|---|
| AC1: 2 variants exist | `cargo test -p fw-save --lib smoke::v0_and_v1_variants_construct` | `SaveEnvelope::V0(SaveV0 {..})` + `SaveEnvelope::V1(SaveV1 {..})` both compile + roundtrip via `encode`/`decode` |
| AC2: V0 → V1 migration preserves seed | `cargo test -p fw-save --lib migration::forward_v0_to_v1_preserves_seed_and_defaults_new_fields` | `migrate_v0_to_v1(SaveV0 { career_seed: Seed::from_u64(42) }) == SaveV1 { career_seed: 42, content_pack_version: 1, ledger: empty }` |
| AC3: load_envelope migrates transparently | `cargo test -p fw-save --lib migration::load_envelope_returns_v1_for_v0_bytes` | `load_envelope(encode(V0(x))) → Ok(SaveV1 { career_seed: x.career_seed, ... })`; `load_envelope(encode(V1(y))) → Ok(y)` |
| AC4a: forward-migration test | Same as AC3 (this IS the forward-migration test) | — |
| AC4b: callback-preservation | `cargo test -p fw-save --lib migration::callback_preservation_v0_seed_survives_migration` | Build V0 with `career_seed = Seed::from_u64(0xDEADBEEF)`; migrate; assert `migrated.career_seed.to_u64() == 0xDEADBEEF` |
| AC4c: forward-incompat-failure | `cargo test -p fw-save --lib migration::load_envelope_rejects_unsupported_future_version` | Hand-craft bytes with bincode tag byte `99`; `load_envelope(...)` returns `Err(SaveError::UnsupportedVersion { discriminant: 99 })` OR `Err(SaveError::Decode(_))` (whichever bincode produces for an unknown variant — verify which) |
| AC4d: round-trip-byte-identical | `cargo test -p fw-save --lib smoke::v1_encode_decode_reencode_produces_identical_bytes` | `let a = encode(env)?; let b = encode(&decode(&a)?)?; assert_eq!(a, b)` |
| AC5: SaveError extension | Same as AC4c | The error variant exists + is constructible + the test assertion compiles against the structured fields |
| AC6: no canonical hash drift | `cargo test -p fw-replay --test canonical_hash` | Both pinned hashes UNCHANGED at commit time (pre-commit canonical-hash-guard.sh exits 0) |

### Files in scope

- `crates/fw-save/src/lib.rs` — extend in-place: add `SaveV0` type + `migrate_v0_to_v1` fn + `load_envelope` fn + `SaveError::UnsupportedVersion` variant + 5 new tests
- `crates/fw-save/Cargo.toml` — likely no new deps (existing serde + bincode + thiserror cover the surface; potentially add `serde_bytes` if hand-crafting the unsupported-version bytes proves tricky)

### Files out of scope (binding)

- `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md`, `docs/DECISIONS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- `docs/MASTER_PLAN.md` (only the T2-9 status flip + done-criteria refinement allowed)
- `design/**.md`
- All other Rust crates (`crates/fw-core/**`, `crates/fw-match-sim/**`, `crates/fw-replay/**`, `crates/fw-memory/**`, `crates/fw-content/**`, `crates/fw-tauri/**`, `crates/fw-content-baker/**`, `crates/fw-scouting/**`)
- `src-tauri/**`, `frontend/**`
- `.claude/**`, `.github/**`, `Justfile`, `rust-toolchain.toml`
- Any pin file: `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs`, fixture RON `expected_hash` fields

### Intentionally NOT done in this task

- Saving the `SeasonState` from T2-5 — the existing SaveV1 design philosophy (per the file header: "Saves are NOT canonical-state-equivalent — they hold the career-level state ... A loaded save replays its match history from the seed to reproduce canonical state on demand") says SeasonState is derived, not saved.
- Tauri IPC commands for save/load — purely the format layer; UI wiring is a separate row.
- File-system persistence (the actual `~/Library/.../saves/<id>.fwsave` path handling) — `encode/decode/load_envelope` are byte-oriented; the file-IO wrapper is a separate row.
- The v1 → v2 migration discipline test landing (T3-7 owns the v1→v2 four-test discipline against a real field-add).
- Authoring `design/specs/save-migration-fixtures.md` — referenced in docs but doesn't exist; would require user input on long-term migration policy + is out-of-scope for /next (use `/log-decision` instead).
- Multi-pack save-fingerprint validation (per Content/RULES.md §6 `mod_load_fingerprint`) — deferred to T3 modding work.

### Plan (trivial: skip chunk decomposition; single-file edit)

Single-file edit with 5 new logical units:
1. `SaveV0` struct (just `career_seed`) + variant `SaveEnvelope::V0(SaveV0)`.
2. `migrate_v0_to_v1(v0: SaveV0) -> SaveV1` — defaults V1's new fields.

4. `SaveError::UnsupportedVersion { discriminant: u32 }` variant.
5. 5 new tests: AC1 smoke (variants construct + encode/decode), AC2 (migration fn correctness), AC3 (load_envelope auto-migrates), AC4b (callback-preservation: distinct seed), AC4c (forward-incompat fails loudly), AC4d (round-trip-byte-identical — re-encode equality).

<!-- ORIGINAL T2-8 SPEC START — pruned 2026-05-18 — kept commented for one cycle of audit traceability
- **id:** T2-8
- **title:** Frontend Transfer-window stub — UI shell only, no transfer mechanics; window-open/closed state derived from current match-day
- **started:** 2026-05-18
- **task class:** UI / frontend (read-only consumption of T2-5 IPC; pure helper function + route rewrite + vitest)
- **required subagent:** none (main thread — well-defined edge cases, no creative design, ~200 LoC including tests)
- **selection note:** T2-7 still BLOCKED on T2-4 (BLOCKED on missing `design/player-generation.md`); T2-8 is the lex-lowest eligible TODO.

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

1. **Window-state visible** — Transfers route renders a clear window-state label: "Summer window — open", "Winter window — open", or "Closed", derived from the current match-day.
2. **State updates with match-day** — calling `getStandings()` returns the current match-day (via `StandingsRow.played` of the first row, same derivation as League page); the window state recomputes whenever standings change.
3. **Determinism rule** — calendar-window mapping is a PURE function `computeTransferWindowState(currentMatchDay: number): WindowState` that lives in `frontend/src/lib/transfer-window.ts`. Rule (simple two-window calendar, deterministic + boundary-tested):
   - `currentMatchDay === 0` → "Summer window — open" (pre-season)
   - `1 <= currentMatchDay <= 18` → "Closed"
   - `19 <= currentMatchDay <= 20` → "Winter window — open"
   - `21 <= currentMatchDay <= 38` → "Closed"
   - `currentMatchDay > 38` → "Closed" (season complete / out-of-bounds defensive)
   The boundary days (19, 20, 21) intentionally encode the FM-style mid-season window per football convention.
4. **"Mechanics deferred" copy** — the page explicitly tells the player transfer mechanics aren't implemented yet; a panel reads "Transfer mechanics land at Phase T3 — bids, negotiations, and contracts." so the empty UI doesn't look broken.
5. **Vitest coverage** — `transfer-window.ts` has unit tests for all 5 branches + each boundary (day 0, 1, 18, 19, 20, 21, 38, 39); the route has a smoke test asserting the window label renders for at least 2 distinct match-days.
6. **No regressions** — `pnpm typecheck` + `pnpm lint` + `pnpm test` stay clean; no Rust files touched.

### AC-to-test matrix

| Acceptance criterion | Exact test or visual check | Observable that proves it |
|---|---|---|
| AC1: window-state label visible | `pnpm test -- Transfers.test.tsx::renders_window_state_label` | Page has element with text matching `/window|closed/i` |
| AC2: state updates with match-day | `pnpm test -- Transfers.test.tsx::window_label_reflects_match_day_via_getStandings` | After `vi.mocked(getStandings).mockResolvedValue([{played: 0, ...}])`, label === "Summer window — open"; after re-render with `played: 19`, label === "Winter window — open" |
| AC3a: pre-season → Summer | `pnpm test -- transfer-window.test.ts::computeTransferWindowState_returns_summer_for_match_day_0` | `computeTransferWindowState(0).kind === "summer"` |
| AC3b: early-season → Closed | Same test file, `..._returns_closed_for_match_day_1_through_18` | `computeTransferWindowState(n).kind === "closed"` for n in [1, 18] |
| AC3c: mid-season → Winter | `..._returns_winter_for_match_day_19_and_20` | `computeTransferWindowState(19).kind === "winter"` AND `computeTransferWindowState(20).kind === "winter"` |
| AC3d: late-season → Closed | `..._returns_closed_for_match_day_21_through_38` | Boundary tested at 21, mid (30), 38 |
| AC3e: out-of-bounds → Closed | `..._returns_closed_for_match_day_above_38` | `computeTransferWindowState(39).kind === "closed"` (defensive) |
| AC4: mechanics-deferred copy | `Transfers.test.tsx::renders_mechanics_deferred_panel` | Page text contains "Transfer mechanics land at Phase T3" |
| AC5: vitest coverage | Same as AC3 + AC1 + AC2 tests | All tests in `transfer-window.test.ts` + `Transfers.test.tsx` pass |
| AC6: no regressions | `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test` + `scripts/fw verify` | All exit 0 |

- `frontend/src/lib/transfer-window.test.ts` — NEW: vitest tests for the helper (8 branch + boundary tests)
- `frontend/src/routes/Transfers.tsx` — REWRITE the placeholder
- `frontend/src/routes/Transfers.test.tsx` — NEW: vitest smoke covering the route render + state derivation

### Files out of scope (binding)

- `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md`, `docs/DECISIONS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- `docs/MASTER_PLAN.md` (only the T2-8 status flip)

### Intentionally NOT done in this task

- Real transfer mechanics (bids, negotiations, contracts) — deferred to Phase T3 explicitly.
- IPC commands for window-state — the derivation is frontend-pure; no backend additions.
- Calendar-date rendering (specific dates like "January 15") — the season concept is match-day-only at T2-5; date conversion would require a new IPC.

- **id:** T2-6
- **title:** Frontend: League page — standings table (TanStack Table v8; sortable columns: P / W / D / L / GF / GA / GD / Pts; dark + light mode) + minimal season action buttons (advance week / play fixtures fast-forward)
- **started:** 2026-05-18
- **task class:** UI / frontend (SolidJS + TanStack Table v8 + Tailwind v3 dark-mode tokens; consumes the T2-5 IPC commands)
- **required subagent:** `ui-programmer` (estimated diff 200-350 LoC across League.tsx replacement + columns file + vitest tests)
- **selection note:** T2-4 (PlayerBio generation) is the first-declared TODO ahead of T2-6 but REMAINS BLOCKED on `design/player-generation.md` authorship; T2-6 picked because it's the next unblocked TODO with all deps DONE (T2-5).

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

1. **8-column sortable standings table** — TanStack Table v8 via the shared `DataTable<T>` component; columns P (played) / W (wins) / D (draws) / L (losses) / GF (goals_for) / GA (goals_against) / GD (goal_difference) / Pts (points). Click any column header sorts asc, click again toggles desc.
2. **Standings data sourced from real IPC** — `routes/League.tsx` calls `getStandings()` from `frontend/src/lib/api/season.ts` via `createResource`; rows render the `StandingsRow` DTO shape; default sort matches the Rust-side canonical sort (points DESC, GD DESC, GF DESC, club_id ASC) so the table opens in the "correct" order even before user interaction.
3. **Dark + light mode both readable** — uses the existing `dark:bg-midnight*` / `dark:text-paper*` / `dark:text-pitch-300` token set; no new Tailwind config changes; passes a manual contrast check against the existing patterns in `components/Layout.tsx` and `components/DataTable.tsx`.
4. **Season action buttons** — `Advance Week` button calls `advanceWeek()`; `Play Fixtures (Fast-Forward)` button calls `playFixtures()`. Both disable + show "Working…" text while pending. On error, IpcError variant message renders inline below the button.
5. **Empty + loading + error states** — initial load shows a loading line; after first `getStandings()` returns 20 zero-played rows, the table renders with those 20 rows; if `getStandings()` throws an IpcError, the page shows the friendly message via the existing `formatIpcError` pattern (or a local copy).
6. **Vitest smoke test** — at minimum: renders the page with a mocked `getStandings()` returning a 3-row fixture; asserts the rendered table has 3 rows + the expected column headers; asserts the "Advance Week" button is present and clickable.
7. **No regressions in existing routes** — `frontend/src/routes/Match.test.tsx` and existing tests stay green; `pnpm typecheck` + `pnpm lint` stay clean.

### AC-to-test matrix

| Acceptance criterion | Exact test or visual check | Observable that proves it |
|---|---|---|
| AC1: 8-column sortable table | `pnpm test -- League.test.tsx::renders_eight_columns_with_correct_headers` | `screen.getAllByRole('columnheader')` length === 8; header text contains "P", "W", "D", "L", "GF", "GA", "GD", "Pts" in order |
| AC2: Standings via getStandings() IPC | `pnpm test -- League.test.tsx::loads_standings_from_ipc_on_mount` | After mount + `vi.mocked(getStandings).mockResolvedValue([...3 rows...])`, the table has exactly 3 `<tr>` rows in `<tbody>` |
| AC3: Dark + light mode readable | Manual eyeball + tailwind class grep | `routes/League.tsx` uses `dark:` variants on every color-bearing class (text + bg); grep for `class=` lines that lack `dark:` returns zero color-bearing matches |
| AC4: Advance week + play fixtures buttons | `pnpm test -- League.test.tsx::advance_week_button_calls_ipc_and_disables_while_pending` | Click "Advance Week" button → `getStandings` mock called twice (initial + post-advance); button disabled with "Working…" text between the two calls |
| AC5: Empty/loading/error states | `pnpm test -- League.test.tsx::shows_friendly_error_when_ipc_throws` | `vi.mocked(getStandings).mockRejectedValue({ kind: 'matchInitFailed', reason: 'x' })` → page renders text containing "match could not start" (formatIpcError output) |
| AC6: Vitest smoke | Same as AC1+AC2 above (one test file covers both) | Tests live at `frontend/src/routes/League.test.tsx` |
| AC7: No regressions | `pnpm test` + `pnpm typecheck` + `pnpm lint` | All exit 0; no new warnings in any existing file |

<!-- ORIGINAL T2-5 SPEC START — pruned 2026-05-18 — kept commented for one cycle of audit traceability
- **id:** T2-5
- **title:** `fw-tauri`: season-controller commands — `advance_week`, `play_fixtures`, `get_standings`, `get_fixtures(club_id)`
- **started:** 2026-05-18
- **task class:** architecture-cross-crate (cross-crate: fw-content + fw-tauri + frontend TS; IPC command surface + new SeasonState type + AppState mutability)
- **required subagent:** `lead-programmer` (estimated diff 600-900 LoC across 4 crates + frontend; main-thread exception thresholds far exceeded)
- **selection note:** picked T2-5 ahead of T2-4 (the first-declared TODO) because T2-4 references `design/player-generation.md` which does NOT exist in the repo — the 22-field gene model + phenotype-label catalog + names→personality mapping are substantive content/balance design calls that require user input via `/log-decision` or design-doc authoring before implementation. T2-5 has clean acceptance criteria + all dependencies (T2-1, T2-2) DONE.

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

1. **Four new Tauri IPC commands ship + serialize cleanly to the frontend**: `advance_week()`, `play_fixtures()`, `get_standings()`, `get_fixtures(club_id)`. Each returns a `Result<Dto, IpcError>` with `#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]` discriminated payloads.
2. **`advance_week()` advances one match-day deterministically**: bumps `SeasonState.current_match_day` by 1; plays all 10 fixtures of the new match-day via the existing `tick_match` loop; records 10 `MatchOutcome` entries in `SeasonState.results`. Same `career_seed` → same outcomes byte-for-byte. Errors if season is already complete (`current_match_day > MATCH_DAYS_PER_SEASON`).
3. **`play_fixtures()` fast-forwards the season**: plays all REMAINING fixtures (current match-day through MATCH_DAYS_PER_SEASON inclusive) in one call. Returns a summary DTO with the count of matches played + final match-day reached.
4. **`get_standings()` returns a sortable standings table**: 20 rows, one per club, with `{club_id, club_name, played, wins, draws, losses, goals_for, goals_against, goal_difference, points}` computed from `SeasonState.results`. Sorted by `(points DESC, goal_difference DESC, goals_for DESC, club_id ASC)` as canonical secondary sort.
5. **`get_fixtures(club_id)` returns all fixtures for a club**: 38 entries (19 home + 19 away), each with `{match_day, opponent_club_id, opponent_club_name, is_home, played, home_score, away_score}`. `played`/`home_score`/`away_score` are `Some(_)` only for fixtures that have been played; `None` otherwise.
6. **Full-season fast-forward completes within 30 seconds** on the dev machine via `cargo test --release` integration test. 380 matches × per-match tick budget (default 600 ticks each = 228k total ticks) must run in <30s.
7. **No canonical hash drift** on either pin — fw-match-sim untouched. fw-tauri + fw-content additions are non-canonical-path; sim crates remain identical.

### AC-to-test matrix

| Acceptance criterion | Exact test or visual check | Observable that proves it |
|---|---|---|
| AC1: 4 commands serialize | `cargo test -p fw-tauri --test season_commands_test::all_four_commands_round_trip_dto_shapes` | Each command's DTO serializes to JSON + the JSON parses back into the DTO; field names are camelCase via `serde_json::Value` field-presence assertions |
| AC2: advance_week deterministic | `cargo test -p fw-tauri --test season_commands_test::advance_week_is_deterministic_for_same_career_seed` | Run advance_week 38 times into AppState A (seed S); run 38 times into AppState B (same seed S); `state_a.results == state_b.results` (BTreeMap of MatchOutcome) |
| AC3: play_fixtures fast-forwards | `cargo test -p fw-tauri --test season_commands_test::play_fixtures_completes_full_season` | After single `play_fixtures()` call: `SeasonState.current_match_day == MATCH_DAYS_PER_SEASON + 1`; `SeasonState.results.len() == MATCHES_PER_SEASON`; AdvanceWeekSummary.matches_played == 380 |
| AC4: get_standings returns 20 sorted rows | `cargo test -p fw-tauri --test season_commands_test::standings_after_full_season_has_20_rows_sorted_by_points_desc` | `standings.len() == 20`; `standings.windows(2).all(|w| (w[0].points, w[0].goal_difference, w[0].goals_for) >= (w[1].points, w[1].goal_difference, w[1].goals_for))`; sum of `wins + draws + losses` across all clubs = 760 (2 × 380); sum of goals_for = sum of goals_against |
| AC5: get_fixtures(club_id) returns 38 entries | `cargo test -p fw-tauri --test season_commands_test::get_fixtures_returns_19_home_plus_19_away_for_each_club` | For each club_id 1..=20: `fixtures.len() == 38`; `fixtures.iter().filter(|f| f.is_home).count() == 19`; same for away; `fixtures.iter().map(|f| f.match_day).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len() == 38` (each match-day appears exactly once) |
| AC6: <30s full season | `cargo test --release -p fw-tauri --test season_commands_test::full_season_perf_under_30s -- --nocapture` | Wall-clock measurement (test uses `std::time::Instant` — non-canonical-path, OK in fw-tauri); `elapsed.as_secs() < 30` |
| AC7: no canonical hash drift | `cargo test -p fw-replay --test canonical_hash` | Both pinned hashes UNCHANGED at commit time (`canonical-hash-guard.sh` hook re-runs at commit + blocks on drift) |

### Files in scope

- `crates/fw-content/src/league.rs` — extend with `SeasonState` + `MatchOutcome` + `StandingsRow` + `Standings` types; pure data + computation methods (`apply_result`, `standings`, `fixtures_for_club`). NO changes to existing `League` / `Fixture` / `generate_*` surface.
- `crates/fw-content/src/lib.rs` — re-export new types.
- `crates/fw-content/tests/season_state_test.rs` — NEW: pure-data unit tests for SeasonState methods (apply_result correctness, standings derivation invariants, fixtures_for_club symmetry).
- `crates/fw-tauri/src/lib.rs` — re-export new DTO types; declare new `season` module.
- `crates/fw-tauri/src/state.rs` — add `season: RwLock<SeasonState>` field to `AppState`; eager-initialize in `AppState::new()` from a config'd career_seed (default `0xfeed_beef_cafe_fade_u64`); add `pub fn season(&self) -> &RwLock<SeasonState>` accessor + `pub fn career_seed(&self) -> Seed` accessor.
- `crates/fw-tauri/src/commands.rs` — add 4 `#[tauri::command]` wrappers + their `_inner` sync helpers (`advance_week_inner`, `play_fixtures_inner`, `get_standings_inner`, `get_fixtures_inner`). Inner fns take `&AppState` (testable without `tauri::State`).
- `crates/fw-tauri/src/season.rs` — NEW module — `play_one_match(seed, content, sig_defs, home_arch, away_arch, ticks) -> MatchOutcome` orchestration helper that wraps the existing `MatchState::initial_with_content` + `tick_match` loop without touching fw-match-sim.
- `crates/fw-tauri/src/error.rs` — extend `IpcError` enum with `SeasonComplete`, `MatchDayNotFound`, `ClubNotFound`, `SeasonNotInitialized` variants if needed.
- `crates/fw-tauri/Cargo.toml` — likely no new deps (existing `std::sync::RwLock` suffices; `std::time::Instant` for the perf test which is fw-tauri-only).
- `crates/fw-tauri/tests/season_commands_test.rs` — NEW: integration tests covering AC2/AC3/AC4/AC5/AC6 against a real `AppState` built from `content/sources/`.
- `src-tauri/src/main.rs` — register the 4 new commands in `tauri::generate_handler![...]`.
- `frontend/src/lib/types.ts` — TS mirror types for `AdvanceWeekSummaryDto`, `StandingsRowDto`, `FixtureWithResultDto`, plus a `MatchOutcomeDto` shared shape.
- `frontend/src/lib/api/season.ts` — NEW: thin `invoke()` wrappers for the 4 commands with TypeScript types. If `frontend/src/lib/api/` doesn't exist, create it; otherwise extend the existing module.

### Files out of scope (binding; pause + escalate if needed)

- `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md`, `docs/DECISIONS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- `docs/MASTER_PLAN.md` (only the T2-5 status flip + done-criteria refinement allowed)
- `design/**.md`
- `crates/fw-match-sim/**`, `crates/fw-replay/**`, `crates/fw-save/**`, `crates/fw-memory/**`, `crates/fw-core/**` (canonical-state + sim crates; T2-5 must use existing public surface only — `tick_match`, `MatchState::initial_with_content`, `MatchState::home_score`/`away_score`)
- `crates/fw-content/src/{player,role_affinity,signature,manager,archetype,team,procgen,markov,runtime,commentary,event}.rs` (existing fw-content types; only `league.rs` + `lib.rs` re-exports may be touched)

### Intentionally NOT done in this task

- League/Squad **page rendering** (T2-6 + T2-7 own the SolidJS components; T2-5 ships only the IPC layer + TS wrappers).
- Match LENGTH realism — using 600 ticks per match (matches the existing canonical-pin tick budget; a full 90-min real-time match at 60Hz would be 324k ticks per match and is out of scope for season-sim perf budget).
- Save / load season state across app restarts (T2-9 owns bincode-based save format).
- Rest weeks, cup matches, international breaks, multi-tier leagues — single league, 38 match-days, no calendar gaps.
- Multi-season support — single `SeasonState` per AppState lifetime.
- Player development / progression triggered by match results — T3 owns.
- Real-time fixture-by-fixture UI progress (per-match streaming events) — `advance_week()` blocks until all 10 fixtures of the day are played; frontend gets the consolidated result.
- Newly-authored `play_match_to_outcome` helper in fw-match-sim — orchestration glue stays in fw-tauri to keep canonical-state crates untouched per scope.

### Plan

- [ ] Chunk 2: `fw-content/tests/season_state_test.rs` — verify (a) apply_result accumulates standings correctly across a hand-built 4-match scenario; (b) standings_sum invariants (sum of W+D+L = 2×matches, sum of GF == sum of GA); (c) fixtures_for_club returns 19 home + 19 away with each match-day appearing exactly once; (d) is_complete() flips after all 380 results.

Per-fixture archetype selection via fixture.home/away clubs' archetype ids (drawn from `League.clubs[i].archetype_id`). Each helper returns the appropriate DTO. - **id:** T1-24

- **started:** 2026-05-16
- **task class:** architecture-cross-crate (Python tooling refactor + regression test + 2 doc updates). ~80-120 LoC total: `scripts/fw-hash-pins.py::update_mode` restructured to preflight-all-then-write-all; new Python regression test in `scripts/test-lint-banned-terms.py` companion or new `scripts/test-fw-hash-pins.py`; doc updates in script docstring + `docs/specs/determinism-gate.md §9`. Tooling-only; no canonical-state change.
- **required subagent:** **main thread** — small focused Python refactor against a fully-specified contract (preflight-then-write is a 30-line transformation; regression test is ~50 LoC; doc updates are paragraph-level). Subagent dispatch overhead outweighs benefit for clearly-scoped tooling work.

### Design references

- **Codex post-followup-review Finding #2** (this session, 2026-05-16): `fw-hash-pins --update` tri-state fix at T1-22 closed the silent-success bug but `update_mode` still writes-per-loop. If location 1 writes and location 2 fails preflight, you get partial rebaseline with exit 1 — contradicts the "atomic updater" claim. Codex's framing: "can be the next hardening patch before the next rebaseline" — not blocking; bites only when a real rebaseline cascade hits a stale registry entry.
- **`scripts/fw-hash-pins.py`** — current `update_mode` (lines 295-360 area): loops over `matching_locs`, calls `update_pin` which writes IMMEDIATELY then returns `(changed, is_failure, msg)`. Refactor target: split `update_pin` into `preflight_pin` (read + compute new_text in memory; return `(changed, is_failure, msg, prepared_write: Option[Tuple[Path, str]])`) + a separate write loop that runs ONLY if zero failures in the preflight pass.
- **`scripts/test-lint-banned-terms.py`** — existing Python test infrastructure pattern (subprocess + tempdir + assertEqual on returncode + stdout). T1-22 wired one Python test suite into Justfile + ci.yml; T1-24 follows the same shape — could either extend that file OR add a new sibling. New sibling is cleaner since the test scope differs (lint vs hash-pins).
- **`docs/specs/determinism-gate.md §9`** — T1-22 added the rebaseline-procedure prose; T1-24 should tighten the atomicity claim to "the `--update` mode is genuinely atomic: zero files modified if any pin location's preflight fails."

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

1. **`update_mode` preflight-then-write structure**: refactored to compute all `(path, new_text)` tuples in memory FIRST; abort with no writes on any preflight failure; only if all preflight succeed, write all files in a second loop.
2. **Regression test** simulating multi-location failure: temporarily break one pin location's regex, invoke `scripts/fw hash-pins --update <hash> --seed <seed>` (live, not dry-run), assert ZERO files modified after exit (verify via `git diff`-style or file-content comparison vs pre-state snapshot).
3. **`scripts/fw verify` clean** end-to-end.
4. **Script docstring + determinism-gate.md §9 updated** to document the genuine-atomicity guarantee.

### AC-to-test matrix

| AC | Test or check | Observable |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Preflight-then-write structure | Read updated `update_mode` body | New code shape: preflight loop that fills `prepared_writes: list[(Path, str)]` + checks for failures; only on zero failures does the write loop run |
| 2. Regression test passes | `python3 scripts/test-fw-hash-pins.py` (new file) | New `test_partial_failure_writes_nothing` passes: temporarily mutates one pin file to break regex; invokes update; asserts NO files changed (via file-content hash comparison vs pre-state) |
| 3. scripts/fw verify | `scripts/fw verify` | exit 0 |
| 4. Docs updated | `grep -n "genuinely atomic\|preflight-then-write\|zero files modified" scripts/fw-hash-pins.py docs/specs/determinism-gate.md` | Both files mention the atomicity guarantee |

**Mutation-thinking pre-check** (per /next Step 6 hardening):
- AC1 preflight-then-write: if I forget to actually guard the write loop with a "no failures" check, the regression test will catch it (files would still be modified despite preflight failure). Discriminating.
- AC2 regression test: directly observable — pre-state vs post-state file content comparison. If atomicity breaks, test fails immediately. Discriminating.

### Files in scope

- `scripts/fw-hash-pins.py` (MODIFIED — `update_pin` → `preflight_pin` return shape; `update_mode` restructured to preflight-all-then-write-all; docstring updated)
- `scripts/test-fw-hash-pins.py` (NEW — Python regression test for atomicity)
- `Justfile` (MODIFIED — `banned-terms` recipe extended to also run `test-fw-hash-pins.py` after `test-lint-banned-terms.py`)
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (MODIFIED — new CI step running `scripts/test-fw-hash-pins.py` on all 3 OSes)
- `docs/specs/determinism-gate.md` (MODIFIED — §9 atomicity claim tightened)

### Files out of scope (do NOT touch — escalate if needed)

- `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs` (T1-22 work; the env vars + helper stay)
- `crates/fw-replay/fixtures/*.ron` (no rebaseline)

- `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md` / `CLAUDE.md` / `docs/MASTER_PLAN.md` row bodies (T1-24 row status flip is the only allowed MASTER_PLAN mutation)
- Frontend / Tauri / content fixture paths

### Intentionally NOT done in this task

- **Convert `update_pin` to return a sealed-enum status** (P2 from T1-22 type-design review) — still defer; the tri-state tuple works for now.
- **Convert `fixtures_load.rs::EXPECTED` raw byte array → `hex!()` macro** (P2 from T1-22 silent-failure review on format-locked byte_array layout) — defer; not a Codex Finding #2 concern.
- **Add `--check` mode** (cross-sibling consistency check; would prevent the inconsistency the script's list mode already detects) — defer.

### Plan (3 chunks — small task)

Phase 1: loop over matching_locs, call `preflight_pin(loc, new_hash)` which returns `(changed, is_failure, msg, prepared: Optional[Tuple[Path, str]])`; accumulate failures + writes. Phase 2: if failures > 0, print all per-location messages (including which would-have-written) + return 1 with ZERO file modifications; else apply all prepared writes in a second loop + return 0. - [ ] **Chunk 3 — Update determinism-gate.md §9 + verify + sync + commit**: update §9 atomicity prose; run `scripts/fw verify`; sync ledgers; atomic commit.

### After implementation

1. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0 — includes new Python test suite.
2. Manual atomicity verification — run the regression scenario standalone to confirm zero-writes-on-failure.

### Design references

Finding #1: T1-21 made `Tick + Tick` / `Tick - Tick` panic-on-overflow, but production cooldown paths use `tick.to_raw() ± ...` directly — bypassing the policy. Finding #3: `dispatch.rs:42` module header still says "Pre-emption hooks: stubbed `None`" when `preempt_check` is now the live 3-policy implementation per T1-19 + ADR-0006 amendment.
- **`crates/fw-core/src/tick.rs`** — T1-21 added `Tick::clamping_add` / `Tick::clamping_sub` opt-in saturation. T1-23 adds two MORE typed helpers for the cooldown-math idiom: `checked_elapsed_since(entry) -> u32` (returns elapsed-tick count; panics if `self < entry`) + `checked_add_ticks(n: u32) -> Tick` (panics on i64 overflow). Per Sim/RULES.md §11 these are panic-on-overflow, NOT opt-in saturation.
- **4 raw-arithmetic callsites to refactor:**
  - `crates/fw-match-sim/src/tactic_fsm.rs:313` — `(now_tick.to_raw() - current.entry_tick.to_raw())` compared against `EVERY_TICKS` cooldown — should be `now_tick.checked_elapsed_since(current.entry_tick)`.
  - `crates/fw-match-sim/src/tactic_fsm.rs:406` — `let ticks_in_state = now_tick.to_raw() - current.entry_tick.to_raw();` — same pattern; same fix.
  - `crates/fw-match-sim/src/dispatch.rs:332` — `Tick::from_raw(state.tick.to_raw() + n as i64)` where `n: u32` from `CooldownPolicy::EveryTicks(u32)` — should be `state.tick.checked_add_ticks(n)`.
  - `crates/fw-match-sim/src/signature/mod.rs:126` — `let end_raw = self.start_tick.to_raw() + self.duration_ticks as i64;` (used as i64 for an unrelated comparison; check whether refactor returns Tick or whether end_raw stays i64 with a `.to_raw()` at the end).

1. **`Tick::checked_elapsed_since(entry: Tick) -> u32`** exists in `fw-core::tick`; panics with informative message if `self < entry` (cooldown invariant violation); returns u32 elapsed-tick-count for valid inputs.

### T1-15 (CLOSED — kept for one cycle of traceability)

- **id:** T1-15
- **title:** Investigate + fix offensive build-up — close T1 exit-gate Bullet 1 (`/done` blocker, 2026-05-16)
- **started:** 2026-05-16
- **task class:** sim-rust (BT leaf utility target fix in `fw-match-sim/src/bt/on_ball.rs`; ≥100 LoC delta expected from cascade — insta snapshots + canonical hash rebaseline on both pins + downstream test fixups)
- **required subagent:** `gameplay-programmer` per CLAUDE.md §5 (sim-Rust ≥100 LoC in fw-match-sim; BT/utility surface)
- **TDD mandate:** the FIX is mechanical (target coordinate change in 1 function) + the falsifiable acceptance is empirical (run 600-tick smoke, count goals). Pure RED-GREEN-REFACTOR doesn't map cleanly because the "test" is the live empirical run on the smoke seed, not a unit test. The unit test in on_ball.rs that pins the OLD target shape (`assert_eq!(target_x, fx)`, `assert_eq!(target_y, fy + 5)`) MUST update to the new shape — that update IS the RED-to-GREEN step in the spirit of TDD. Cite the exemption in the commit body per Superpowers TDD skill mandate (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-13).
- **Canonical-hash rebaseline:** **YES — AUTHORIZED** per the T1-15 row's own acceptance criterion + per ADR-0012 trigger #3 (sim behavior change with documented intent). BOTH pins WILL shift: 60-tick smoke `ddccaf88…000b3` + 600-tick extended `66585ca8…4625` → new values. Update `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` + `PINNED_600_TICK` + `crates/fw-replay/fixtures/0xdeadbeefdeadbeef.ron::expected_hash` + `crates/fw-replay/fixtures/0xfeedbeefcafefade.ron::expected_hash` + `crates/fw-content/tests/fixtures_load.rs::EXPECTED` in the same commit per `docs/specs/determinism-gate.md` §9. Append re-baseline history entries in both fixture files + the in-code const doc-comments.

### Design references

- **`crates/fw-match-sim/src/bt/on_ball.rs:242-244`** — `utility_pass_short` target generation. The bug: `let target_x = fx; let target_y = fy + Q32::from_int(5);` — target is the passer's own formation X column with a 5m sideways offset, NOT toward the opponent's goal. Compare:
  - `utility_pass_long` (line 276-281): `target_x = if home { Q32::from_int(25) } else { Q32::from_int(-25) }; target_y = fy` — correctly advances 25m forward.
  - `utility_cross` (line 312-316): `target_x = if home { Q32::from_int(40) } else { Q32::from_int(-40) }; target_y = ZERO` — correctly advances to attacking third + targets goal centre.
  - `utility_dribble` (line 342-348): `target_x = if home { fx + Q32::from_int(8) } else { fx - Q32::from_int(8) }; target_y = fy` — correctly advances 8m forward.
  - `utility_lay_off` (line 401-403): `target_x = fx; target_y = fy - Q32::from_int(7)` — INTENTIONALLY backward (lay-off is a layback to a supporting runner; correct behavior).
  - `utility_hold_ball` (line 376-378): `target = formation_position(roster_slot)` — passer's own position; correct (hold doesn't release the ball).
  Short-pass is the OUTLIER + the most-picked pass leaf in steady-state midfield play.
- **`crates/fw-match-sim/src/dispatch.rs:800` — `nearest_teammate_near`** scans the passer's team for the teammate with minimum Manhattan distance to the supplied `(target_x, target_y)`. With short-pass's bad target (passer's own column), it always finds an adjacent teammate at similar formation depth → ball loops within the same X-column forever. No bug in `nearest_teammate_near`; it does what it's told.
- **`crates/fw-core/src/lib.rs`** — pitch dimensions: `PITCH_LENGTH_M = 105` (X axis), `PITCH_WIDTH_M = 68` (Y axis), `GOAL_LINE_X = 52.5` (positive for away goal; home goal at -52.5). Forward direction for home team = increasing X; forward for away = decreasing X.
- T1 exit-gate Bullet 1 envelope: "2-5 goals total across the 600 ticks, no NaN-tier weirdness."
- Empirical diagnostic from `/done` attempt (2026-05-16, ddcdfdf): 5 seeds sampled = 0 goals every run; ball X-range [0, 2.64m]; possession sits with home slots 8/9/10 the entire 600-tick run.

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

1. **600-tick run on smoke seed `0xfeedbeefcafefade` produces 2-5 goals** (T1 exit gate Bullet 1 envelope met).
2. **`ball.pos_x` absolute exceeds 30m in ≥1 frame** across the 600 ticks (proves the ball reaches the attacking third).
3. **≥1 `MatchEvent::Shot` emitted** in the smoke run (proves the shot-attempt utility fires when ball is in range).
4. **Sanity check: 4 additional seeds** (`0xdeadbeefdeadbeef` / `0xcafef00dcafef00d` / `0x0123456789abcdef` / `0xabcdef0123456789`) — each produces 0-7 goals (a wider sanity envelope; the smoke seed is the locked 2-5; the other 4 just need to be in a reasonable range, not all 0 + not all 10+).
5. **Canonical hash REBASELINED for BOTH pins** (60-tick smoke `ddccaf88…000b3` + 600-tick extended `66585ca8…4625` → new BLAKE3 values). Updated in 4 locations per Step 7.5 of the determinism-gate spec: `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` + `PINNED_600_TICK`; `crates/fw-replay/fixtures/0xdeadbeefdeadbeef.ron::expected_hash`; `crates/fw-replay/fixtures/0xfeedbeefcafefade.ron::expected_hash`; `crates/fw-content/tests/fixtures_load.rs::EXPECTED` if present. Re-baseline history entries appended in fixture file headers + const doc-comments.
6. **`scripts/fw verify` green** end-to-end including rebaselined hashes.
7. **No regression in any of the other 4 T1 exit-gate bullets** that passed at /done attempt — 5 behavioral proptests still hold over 100 random seeds; replay corpus drift-detectors pass with the new pins; cargo test + clippy + fmt clean; no `unwrap()` regression in `fw-match-sim/src/` non-test code; commentary still renders position + decision context.

T1-9 cites these.

- `crates/fw-core/src/lib.rs::PITCH_WIDTH_M = 68`, `SIDELINE_Y = ±34` — team-width bound reference for the "team width in [35, 65] m" invariant.
- `crates/fw-core/src/tick.rs::TICKS_PER_SECOND = 60` — used to derive "4 seconds = 240 ticks" for the sustained-sprint window.
- `crates/fw-match-sim/src/dispatch.rs::MAX_PLAYER_SPEED = 5.0 m/s` (module-private const) — current sim caps player speed at 5 m/s; the "no sustained sprint >12 m/s for 4s" invariant is therefore VACUOUSLY TRUE today + future-proof against a MAX_PLAYER_SPEED bump without throttle update. Cite this rationale in the test doc-comment.
- `MatchState::possession()` accessor (T1-3.5) — returns `Option<PlayerSlot>` for current ball carrier. Slot 0..11 = home team; slot 11..22 = away team. Drives the team-in-possession dimension for the team-width invariant.

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable)

**T1-9 scope per ADR-0007 §Layer 3** — interpreted against the current T1 sim (no per-team archetype loading; that's T2-1):

1. New `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/behavior_proptest.rs` exists + compiles + is `cargo test` discoverable.
2. **Positional invariant (a-1)** `gk_home_stays_near_own_goal_95pct_of_ticks` — proptest over `arb_seed()` (100 cases default). For each seed, runs `MatchState::initial(seed)` + 60 ticks of `tick_match`, then asserts: home GK (slot 0) is within 30m of own goal line (x = -52.5) for ≥ 95% of the 61 sampled ticks (including tick 0). Distance = absolute X-delta plus Y-extent envelope (simpler than Euclidean — use `|pos_x - (-52.5)| ≤ 30` as the gate; tightens to 2D distance in T2). Anti-vacuous: also asserts the GK position actually evolves (initial.pos != tick_60.pos) so a no-op tick_match would fail the test.
3. **Positional invariant (a-2)** `gk_away_stays_near_own_goal_95pct_of_ticks` — mirror of (a-1) for slot 11 against +52.5 goal line.
4. **Positional invariant (a-3)** `team_width_when_in_possession_within_band` — proptest over `arb_seed()` × 60 ticks. For each tick where `state.possession.is_some()`, compute the carrier's team's outfield Y-range (max - min of pos_y across slots 1..11 if home / 12..22 if away — excluding GK). Assert range is in [25, 70] meters across **every** sampled tick. The band is wider than ADR-0007's [35, 65] envelope on purpose — T1 doesn't load tactical archetypes, so team width is driven by BT routing + formation positions alone; the wider envelope is a regression-guard that catches collapse-to-single-point or spread-to-sidelines pathologies + tightens in T2-1 when archetypes load. Anti-vacuous: also asserts the test observed `≥ 1` in-possession tick across the run (else the assertion is vacuously satisfied over zero samples).
5. **Positional invariant (a-4)** `no_player_sustained_sprint_over_threshold_for_4_seconds` — proptest over `arb_seed()` × `MIN(600, total_ticks)` ticks (we need ≥240 ticks to detect a 4-second sustained sprint). For each player + each 240-tick sliding window, assert no window has every tick's speed > 12 m/s. Today VACUOUSLY TRUE because `MAX_PLAYER_SPEED = 5.0 m/s` in dispatch.rs clamps below 12; future-proof against a cap-bump without corresponding throttle. Test doc-comment cites the regression-guard rationale + the current 5.0 m/s constant for context.
6. **PlayerSeparation invariants (b)** — DELEGATED to existing `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/separation_proptest.rs` (inv1 through inv6b shipped at T1-2b-iii-d). The doc-comment header in `behavior_proptest.rs` explicitly cites: "PlayerSeparation invariants ship in separation_proptest.rs (inv6 + inv6b cover the 0.4m floor over time)". No new tests authored under (b); the existing 8 invariants exceed the row's required coverage (the inv6b 2-consecutive-tick limit is stricter than the row's "30 consecutive ticks" framing).
7. **events_chronological (d)** — DELEGATED to existing `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/match_event_proptest.rs::events_chronological` (shipped at T1-4a). The doc-comment header cites this.
8. **Deferred to T2-1 (no per-team archetype loading)**: positional invariant (a-4 in original row) defender-depth-tracks-archetype + all 3 pair-seed knob-isolation tests under (c). Cite in commit body + behavior_proptest.rs header doc-comment with explicit T2-1 deferral note.
9. **Deferred to T2 per ADR-0007** (already deferred in MASTER_PLAN row): 5 stat-distribution assertions.
10. `scripts/fw verify` green end-to-end including the new behavior_proptest.rs invariants.
11. Proptest CASES env-var defaults (256 cases per invariant) keep total runtime ≤30 seconds for the new file — tight but feasible since each case runs ≤60 ticks except (a-4)'s 240-tick window which runs 240 ticks.

### Acceptance criteria (falsifiable; mapped to Codex's findings)

1. **Codex's exact criterion**: 600-tick run with `MatchState::initial_with_content` produces (a) ≥1 `MatchEvent::Pass` OR `MatchEvent::Shot` in `match_events` AND (b) `ball.pos_x != 0.0` OR `ball.pos_y != 0.0` at tick 600. New `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/normal_path_ball_action_test.rs` (NEW) asserts both.
2. `role_states::evaluate_transitions` reads `state.possession`: when the player's slot matches `state.possession`, transition to `InPossession` (outfield) or `GoalkeeperState::DistributingFromHand` (GK slot 0 / 11). When the player's slot is NOT the carrier, transition back to the appropriate non-possession state (existing role-derived state). Unit tests in role_states.rs cover both branches.
3. **`MatchFrameDto.possession: Option<u8>`** field added. `from_state` projection copies `state.possession`. TS `MatchFrameDTO` mirror gets `possession: number | null`. The dev-board `Dev/TacticalBoard.tsx` could optionally visualize the carrier (defer to T4-1; for T1-3.6 just ship the field).
4. **Canonical-hash REBASELINED** from `blake3:782fcde65ba8a0fc12bb90af1b61f77d8cd403103ab3671b0d5d6b03e75c8c0f` to whatever the new BT-routed-ball-motion smoke seed produces. Update `PINNED_60_TICK` const in `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs` + `0xdeadbeefdeadbeef.ron` corpus fixture + `fw-content/tests/fixtures_load.rs::EXPECTED` if present. Documented as authorized in this MEMORY spec + the commit body cites ADR-0012 trigger #1.
5. **Canonical unique-attribute test**: new `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/canonical_unique_attr_test.rs` (NEW). Constructs a PlayerAttributes fixture where all 55 fields have distinct raw bit values (`Q32::from_raw(i)` for i in 1..56, or similar). Computes the canonical hash. Then iterates over every pair of distinct field positions (i, j) where i < j, swaps the two fields' values in a new fixture, recomputes the hash, and asserts the swapped-hash != original-hash. 55 choose 2 = 1485 swaps — feasible at test time. Catches the canonical-encoding hole Codex found.
6. **Frontend runtime shape guards**: new `frontend/src/lib/runtime-validators.ts` (NEW) with `isMatchResult(v: unknown): v is MatchResult` + `isMatchFrameDTO(v: unknown): v is MatchFrameDTO` + `isBackendHandshake(v: unknown): v is BackendHandshake`. New `safeInvoke<T>(cmd, args, guard): Promise<T>` wrapper in `tauri.ts` that calls `invoke()` + runs the guard + throws a typed `IpcShapeError` on guard fail. Migrate `playMatch` + `match_frames` + `getBackendHandshake` to use `safeInvoke`. Test in `frontend/src/lib/runtime-validators.test.ts` (NEW) asserts each guard rejects 2-3 malformed payloads with specific shape distortions.
7. **Stale-doc cleanup**: MEMORY.md line 3 rewritten from "Next: T1-4" to reflect current state (e.g. "Phase T1: 7 of 11 numbered rows DONE; T1-3.6 audit-response in flight; T1-8 + T1-9 remain"). MASTER_PLAN.md "## Snapshot (2026-05-13)" header rewritten to "## Snapshot (2026-05-16)" + body refreshed to mention the audit-triage P1 sweep closure + T1-3.6 audit-response.
8. `scripts/fw verify` green end-to-end post-implementation + post-self-review. Canonical hash REBASELINED + pinned. `dump_frames --content content --ticks 600` produces frames where ball moves in ≥1 of them (manually re-verify Codex's reproduction).
9. `pnpm test` continues green (34 → ~37 tests with new runtime-validator tests).

- 2026-06-02 — **T4-F1 — breakthrough-delta RNG now keys on the GLOBAL career `EventId`, not the renumbered batch index (external-review correctness fix; pillar 3).** `sim-rust` (fw-memory + fw-tauri; NON-canonical — both pins UNCHANGED), `gameplay-programmer` under TDD. **Origin:** 2026-06-02 external dual review F1 (CONFIRMED + main-thread re-verified); DECISIONS 2026-06-02 external-review entry; first task of the re-prioritised career-layer (correct → persistent → visible) sequence. Out-of-cycle (not a `/next` pick — human relayed an external review). **The bug:** `evaluate()` seeded the delta RNG with `tick_for_rng = event.event_id.0` (`breakthrough.rs:984`/`:1049`), but `filter_new_events_for_player` (season.rs) `append`ed the per-player batch into a fresh `MemoryLedger` and `append` (`ledger.rs:169`) renumbers ids from 0 → the RNG `tick` was the within-batch index, so a given `(player, family)` at batch-position k drew the IDENTICAL delta every season (site already includes player_id → within-player cross-season correlation, not cross-player). Off the canonical-hash path → CI never caught it; the season.rs comment claiming id-preservation was false. **Fix:** new `pub fn MemoryLedger::from_events(Vec<MemoryEvent>)` — preserves ids verbatim (vs `append`'s renumber), `next_id = max+1`, `dirty=true`, `#[must_use]`, + a §11 `assert!` that input ids are strictly ascending (the precondition `get_by_id`'s binary search + `evaluate`'s chronological order rely on). Both filters (`filter_new_events_for_player` prod + `filter_ledger_for_player` test-only) rewritten to collect → `from_events`; both gates (positive + regressive) now read the global id automatically; false comment + stale `sample_positive_delta` `tick` doc corrected. **Self-review triple → Accept + fixes (all landed):** (P1, silent-failure + type-design CONVERGENT) `from_events` produced gappy-id ledgers but `get_by_id` resolved by ARRAY POSITION (`events.get(id.0)`) → silent wrong/None on a view + the index-readers round-trip through it (a §11 fail-open trap, dormant today since `evaluate` uses `iter()` only) → FIXED by resolving `get_by_id` by id (binary search; behaviour-identical for append-built contiguous ledgers, correct for gappy views) + the ascending-id assert makes compact/append on a view consistent too. (P1, code-reviewer) stale `sample_positive_delta` doc said `tick` is "the in-game tick" → corrected to the global `EventId`. (P2) `#[must_use]` + `+1` overflow-intent note; AC3 (`filter_ledger_for_player`) now asserts id-preservation (was count/attribution only). **Tests:** `from_events_preserves_ids_and_sets_next_id` + `_empty_has_next_id_zero` (fw-memory); `different_event_ids_produce_different_deltas_f1` (asserts `delta(id=50)≠delta(id=100)` AND both ≠ the id=0 pre-fix value 7, + determinism same-id) ; `filter_new_events_for_player_preserves_global_event_ids` (production path → ids [2,3] not [0,1]) + the AC3 id assertions. **Canonical pins UNCHANGED** (`85f45bf8…`/`206bddae…`) — fw-memory off the match-hash path. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0; all fw-memory (69 lib) + fw-tauri (85 lib + 10 breakthrough_wiring) green. **MUST precede T4-2.5g** (SaveV4 would otherwise persist correlated progression). - 2026-05-21 — **T3-R-F Career clock for salience decay — `CareerState::current_tick()` replaces the `Tick::ZERO` placeholder.** Sixth and FINAL row of the post-T3 cleanup cluster (ultimate-review Track A P2). Main thread (single-crate `fw-tauri`, ~190 LoC mostly tests); RED-GREEN TDD. **Problem**: `get_player_detail_inner`'s `SalienceReader::top_n` call hard-coded `now_tick = Tick::ZERO`, which silently DISABLED salience decay entirely (`project_salience`'s `elapsed <= 0` guard always fired) — a dead feature with a `// TODO(T4)` marker. **Ships**: NEW derived `CareerState::current_tick() -> Tick` (state.rs) — a monotonic career clock = `(season_number × MATCH_DAYS_PER_SEASON + current_match_day) × SEASON_MATCH_TICK_BUDGET`, in the per-match `Tick` unit. **Design fork resolved**: derived (a pure fn of the canonical `season_number`/`current_match_day`), NOT a stored field — a stored clock would need `advance_season` maintenance + risks desync. Monotonic non-decreasing + continuous across a season rollover (at season-N-complete `current_match_day == MATCH_DAYS+1`, the value equals season-(N+1)-day-1 — verified by a test). No new tuning constant, no new mutable state, no `advance_season` change. `get_player_detail_inner` now computes `let now_tick = career.current_tick();` BEFORE the `&mut career.ledger` borrow (avoids a borrow conflict — `current_tick()` needs `&career`) and passes it to `top_n`; `Tick` dropped from the `fw_core` import. The row named `get_player_detail` / `get_career_overview` but `get_career_overview_inner` has no salience-reader call — there was exactly one `Tick::ZERO` site. **Tests**: 3 `current_tick` unit tests (non-zero for a fresh career; advances by exactly `SEASON_MATCH_TICK_BUDGET` per match-day; rollover-continuous) + `salience_decay_applied_through_career_clock` integration test — two same-raw-salience events for a player (one `Linear`-decay, one `Never`); at the career clock the decayer is fully decayed so the `Never` event ranks first, at `Tick::ZERO` they tie → lower `event_id` first → order flips, proving the career clock genuinely feeds decay. **Self-review triple → 3× ACCEPT**, no P0/P1. **Canonical hashes UNCHANGED** — `fw-tauri` is not a canonical-state crate. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0. **P3 follow-up** (type-design-analyzer, explicitly "speculative for one call site — flag if a second consumer appears"): a distinct `CareerTick` newtype would prevent mixing a career-clock `Tick` with a per-match sim `Tick`; deferred per CLAUDE.md §7 (no speculative abstraction). **The post-T3 ultimate-review cleanup cluster (T3-R-A..F) is now COMPLETE** — next `/next` picks the first Phase T4 row.

The skipped-emission paths intentionally do NOT reset the readiness/pressure meter — it stays at threshold so a player whose PA later un-caps via a regressive collapse fires the breakthrough they earned. **Dead code**: the unused `pub const CA_LIFT_FRACTION` + its `lib.rs` re-export deleted; `compute_ca_delta`'s comment rewritten to honestly describe its integer `delta_pa / 2`. **Tests**: 5 new — `evaluate_at_pa_ceiling_skips_without_panic`, `evaluate_at_career_floor_skips_without_panic`, `gate_respects_min_stakes_floor` (boundary test — kills `>=`→`>`), `positive_kind_prefers_signature_over_flag` (kills the Kind-1/Kind-2 priority flip), + proptest `evaluate_never_panics_for_any_pa_ca` (well-formed `ca = min(a,b)` / `pa = max(a,b)`; the `a=1,b=1` regression seed is committed). **Self-review triple → 2 ACCEPT + 1 ACCEPT-with-finding**: the code-reviewer's "Important" (conf. 92) — meter-not-reset means re-evaluation while PA stays at 200 — was assessed as **not a bug**: its premise (PA can never un-cap) ignores regressive collapse; meter-stays is the correct semantics (preserves earned readiness for post-collapse recovery). The skip comment was clarified to name the un-cap mechanism; type-design-analyzer's "exact"→"deterministic" wording note applied. **Canonical match-state hashes UNCHANGED** — `evaluate` is not on a canonical-hash path. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0. - 2026-05-18 — **T2-9 `fw-save` bincode-based save format + V0→V1 version-migration enum chain — first schema version LOCKED at `1`.** Final T2 MVP row before T2-10 phase-gate Codex review. **Ships**: extended `crates/fw-save/src/lib.rs` (~260 LoC added). NEW `SaveEnvelope::V0(SaveV0) = 0` variant (fictional pre-T2-9 stub, fixture-only — kept FOREVER to exercise migration discipline) + existing `SaveEnvelope::V1(SaveV1) = 1` (T2-9-locked first real schema with `career_seed` + `content_pack_version` + `ledger`). `#[repr(u32)]` + EXPLICIT discriminants on the enum so any future variant-reorder fails to compile (Rust forbids duplicate explicit discriminants). NEW `migrate_v0_to_v1(v0) -> SaveV1` pure forward-migration fn supplying defaults for V1-only fields (`content_pack_version=1`, `ledger=MemoryLedger::new()`). NEW `load_envelope(bytes) -> Result<SaveV1, SaveError>` production entry point — decodes envelope + auto-migrates V0 → V1 + propagates unknown discriminants as `SaveError::Decode` (bincode's serde-adapter rejection). NEW `SaveError::TrailingBytes { consumed, total }` variant + `decode()` now rejects trailing bytes past the envelope (post-T2-9 silent-failure-hunter P1 fix — prior shape silently accepted corrupted/spliced saves). **11 tests (9 new)** covering: forward-migration (V0→V1 preserves seed + defaults new fields); callback-preservation (bit-exact seed survival on a non-trivial value); forward-incompat-failure (V99 bytes load fails via `SaveError::Decode` with BOTH "99" AND "variant" in message — strict dual-substring assertion); round-trip-byte-identical (encode→decode→re-encode produces identical bytes); load_envelope auto-migration both directions; V0 + V1 both construct + round-trip cleanly; **2 wire-byte regression tests pinning V0 at `0x00` + V1 at `0x01`** (post-T2-9 type-design P0 fix — schema is locked FOREVER so the wire bytes are part of the lock); trailing-bytes rejection. **Self-review triple: 2 P0 + 4 P1 + 4 P2/P3 — all 6 P0/P1 fixed in-place pre-commit per fail-closed discipline (fw-save is canonical-state-adjacent)**. silent-failure-hunter REVISE: (P1) `decode()` silently discarded `_consumed` from `decode_from_slice` → corrupted save with appended garbage would load cleanly as its prefix; FIX: check `consumed == bytes.len()` + new `SaveError::TrailingBytes`. (P1) forward-incompat test was too permissive (either-or substring) — recommended tighter pattern-match on `DecodeError::UnexpectedVariant`, BUT empirical bincode-2-with-serde produces `DecodeError::OtherString("invalid value: integer \`99\`, expected variant index 0 <= i < 2")` not the structured variant; FIX: tightened to require BOTH "99" AND "variant" substrings (strictly stronger than original OR check; loud failure if either token disappears in a future bincode release). type-design REVISE: (P0) variant-tag-drift unprotected (doc-comment-only) → future rustfmt/refactor reordering V0⇄V1 silently swaps every save in the wild; FIX: `#[repr(u32)]` + explicit `V0(...) = 0, V1(...) = 1` discriminants make reordering a visible diff + compile-rejected if duplicates appear. (P0) missing wire-byte regression test → existing smoke test only asserted V0/V1 bytes DIFFER, not WHICH bytes; a tag swap would still pass; FIX: 2 new tests pinning `bytes[0] == 0x00` for V0 + `bytes[0] == 0x01` for V1. code-reviewer REVISE confirmed both silent-failure findings (already addressed) + flagged 2 P1 doc/naming items deferred. 4 P2/P3 deferred to T3-7 multi-version chain or follow-up: ContentPackVersion(NonZeroU32) newtype belongs in fw-core (out of scope); SaveLatest type alias for `pub type SaveLatest = SaveV1;`; `#[must_use]` on `migrate_v0_to_v1`; `load_envelope` empty-bytes behavior doc. **Canonical hashes UNCHANGED on both pins** — fw-save is non-canonical-state-pin path; sim crates untouched. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0 end-to-end. **PHASE T2 COMPLETE**: 10 of 10 MVP rows DONE; user runs `/done` next to open the T2-10 phase-gate Codex review PR.
