Added `: MatchFrameDTO` + `: MatchFrameDTO[]` return type annotations with comments explaining the drift-detection rationale. **Deferred to commit body (analysts recommended accept-as-is):** silent-failure P3 (?source=Tauri message check + ?ticks=-1 boundary + fwDev DEV-gate doc — low-priority polish); type-design P3 (mockSearchParams Partial<Record<string,string>> mirror — current Record<string,string> coerces fine via URLSearchParams; Pixi mock cast strategy — known Vitest tradeoff; deny.toml allowlist narrowing — defer to T2+ when dep policy needs tightening). **Cross-crate scope expansion**: `Cargo.toml` workspace.package license string change (SPDX-valid form); no new workspace deps. ~190 LoC source delta (~80 deny.toml + 33 Justfile + 71 ci.yml + 6 Cargo.toml license edit + 41 fw-dev.d.ts NEW); 2 new test files (~250 LoC FrameSource + ~210 LoC TacticalBoard = ~460 LoC tests); 9 files changed end-to-end (2 NEW test + 1 NEW deny.toml + 1 NEW fw-dev.d.ts + Justfile + ci.yml + Cargo.toml + 2 SUT minor-touched [TacticalBoard.tsx fwDev type + FrameSource.test.ts fixture type]). `pnpm test` baseline: 34 tests / 3 files / ~830ms (acceptance criterion <10s — 12x headroom). **The Codex 2026-05-16 audit-triage P1 sweep is now COMPLETE.** T1 phase remaining: T1-7 procgen + T1-8 replay corpus + T1-9 behavioral assertions (all non-P1 polish). **Next: T1-7** per MASTER_PLAN order (first non-P1 row) OR T1-9 behavioral assertions (substantive but lower priority).
- 2026-05-16 — **T1-10 fw-core::math: replace runtime LazyLock LUT bake with committed const Q32 tables (Codex 2026-05-16 audit P1 closure — 4th of 5 audit-triage P1s).** Closes "Runtime f64 LUT bake is a determinism risk." Previously `SIGMOID_LUT` + `EXP_LUT` (257 entries each) were built at process startup via `LazyLock<[Q32; 257]>` using `f64::exp()` + quantized to Q32 — libm/platform-dependent by design. Cross-OS hash passed today but a future libc/glibc/macOS-libsystem change in `exp()`'s ULP behavior at one of 257 LUT points could silently drift the bake + break determinism without any code change. T1-10 commits the baked Q32 raw bits as source byte sequence; drift becomes a code-review artifact not a silent runtime issue. Main-thread implementation (mechanical refactor, behavior-preserving by construction): (chunk 1) one-shot printer test re-bakes via f64 + emits `pub(crate) const ... = [i64; 257]` Rust literals; (chunk 2) new `crates/fw-core/src/math_luts.rs` (NEW, 186 LoC) commits the 514 raw-bits entries (257 SIGMOID + 257 EXP) + header documents bake source + regeneration procedure; (chunk 3) `crates/fw-core/src/math.rs` rewrote: deleted `use std::sync::LazyLock`, both `static LazyLock<[Q32; LUT_N]>`, `DOMAIN_MAX_F64`, `STEP_F64`, the `#[allow(clippy::float_arithmetic)]` constant-attribute; added const-fn helper `q32_array_from_raw(raw: &[i64; LUT_N]) -> [Q32; LUT_N]` using `while i < LUT_N` loop (const-fn-stable; for-range not yet) + `Q32::from_raw` per-entry; new `const SIGMOID_LUT: [Q32; LUT_N] = q32_array_from_raw(&math_luts::SIGMOID_LUT_RAW);` (same for EXP_LUT). Public surface `sigmoid_q32` + `exp_q32` unchanged. (chunk 4) `crates/fw-core/tests/lut_drift_detection.rs` (NEW, `#[ignore]`-gated): re-bakes via the original f64 formulas + asserts each entry equals the committed const bit-for-bit. Diagnostic names index, x, expected/actual raw bits, diff. Header documents "when to run" + the rebaseline-canonical-hash-per-ADR-0012 procedure. Plus `crates/fw-core/src/q32.rs`: promoted `from_f64_clamped` from `pub(crate)` to `pub` so integration tests can call it (the alternative — vendoring rounding logic in the test — would subtly diverge from `fixed::FixedI64::saturating_from_num`'s round-to-nearest-even on 1-bit boundary cases; verified empirically when my first drift-test attempt used truncation and failed at i=0 with a 1-bit difference). `scripts/determinism-audit.py`: REMOVED the now-obsolete `math.rs` file-level exemption (math.rs is now pure Q32 — if f64 reappears the audit fails); ADDED `tests/lut_drift_detection.rs` to the per-rule float exemption (intentional bake-time f64; `#[ignore]`-gated). **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — the committed const values match the prior LazyLock-baked values byte-for-byte (proven by the drift test passing on first run). **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (1 P2 + 2 P3 — stale `pub(crate)` doc-comment at signature/dispatcher.rs:649; printer test self-referential [overlap with code-reviewer P2]; drift-test toolchain context). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT (2 P2 + 3 P3 — SIGMOID_LUT/EXP_LUT `pub(crate)` → module-private; `from_f64_clamped` `#[doc(hidden)]`; [i64;257] vs [Q32;257]; LUT_N duplication; sibling vs nested module — last 3 P3s analyst recommended accept). code-reviewer ACCEPT (1 P2 + 2 P3 — printer self-referential [overlap]; drift-test tautology caveat; q32.rs exemption breadth). **Three clean silent-failure-hunter verdicts in a row** (T1-11 + T1-12 + T1-10) — anti-failure discipline holding. Main-thread fix-pass closed 4 P2 in-place: (P2 silent-failure stale doc-comment) `signature/dispatcher.rs:649` referenced `pub(crate) from_f64_clamped` — now `pub` post T1-10. Rewrote comment to "without calling the bake-time-only `Q32::from_f64_clamped` (T1-10 promoted to `pub` for math-LUT drift-detection but explicitly off-limits to canonical-path code)." (P2 silent-failure + code-reviewer printer self-reference) prior `print_luts_oneshot.rs` called `sigmoid_q32` + `exp_q32` — which read the COMMITTED const back out, defeating the documented "regeneration procedure": after a real drift event the printer would emit stale committed values, not new f64-baked truth. Rewrote printer to share the EXACT f64-bake path with `lut_drift_detection` (`lut_x_f64` + `1.0 / (1.0 + (-x).exp())` + `Q32::from_f64_clamped`); the printer + drift detector now have one source of truth. Verified the rewritten printer produces identical values to the committed const (proves the f64 path matches). Added `print_luts_oneshot.rs` to determinism-audit exemption since it now has intentional bake-time f64. (P2 type-design F P2-1) `SIGMOID_LUT` + `EXP_LUT` were `pub(crate)` — exposed to every fw-core module (player_attributes, seed, ids, etc.) that has no business reading raw LUT tables. Demoted to module-private const (matching the prior `static LazyLock`'s file-local scope); the drift test reads them via public `sigmoid_q32`/`exp_q32` so no test access is lost. (P2 type-design F P2-2) `from_f64_clamped` got `#[doc(hidden)]` so it's still callable from drift tests + future T2-3 baker but doesn't show up in rustdoc — discourages discovery by contributors who haven't read the "BAKE-TIME ONLY" warning. **Deferred to commit body:** silent-failure P3 (drift-test toolchain-context print — low-priority polish); type-design P3 ([i64;257] storage shape, LUT_N duplication, sibling vs nested module — analyst recommended accept-as-is on all three); code-reviewer P3 (drift-test tautology caveat — already documented inline; q32.rs file-level exemption breadth — pre-existing scope, low risk given file size + stability). **Cross-crate scope expansion**: `Q32::from_f64_clamped` `pub(crate)` → `pub` + `#[doc(hidden)]` (single API surface change, drift-test-only consumer + future T2-3 baker). ~150 LoC source delta + ~250 LoC tests (math_luts.rs 186 + lut_drift_detection.rs ~120 + print_luts_oneshot.rs ~95) + ~50 LoC test infrastructure. 8 files changed end-to-end. `scripts/fw verify` clean post fix-pass (cargo fmt + clippy + workspace tests + canonical-hash regression on `782fcde6…8c0f` + banned-terms + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate). **4 of 5 audit-triage P1s now closed** (T1-3.5 ball-actions P0 + T1-11 signatures unreachable + T1-5 IPC split-brain + T1-5 match_frames unbounded + T1-12 content validation + T1-10 LUT bake). **Next: T1-13** — 5th + FINAL audit-triage P1 closure (frontend test gate + cargo audit + cargo deny wired into `just verify` + CI). After T1-13 the full Codex 2026-05-16 audit-triage P1 sweep is complete.

- 2026-05-16 — **Codex Tier-2 audit fix-pass (post-T1-6) — closes 2 P0 IPC shape mismatches Codex caught in the "vertical complete" claim.** Codex Tier-2 verdict was REVISE on the diff range `537148f3..bb2100e3` (T1-11/T1-5/T1-12/T1-6) with 2 P0 findings: (1) `MatchResult.match_events: Vec<fw_content::MatchEvent>` serialised externally-tagged as `{ "KickOff": {...} }` but frontend expected flat `{ tick, minute, kind, description }` → production `pnpm tauri dev` Play→render would have broken; Vitest tests missed it because they mocked events in the FRONTEND shape directly (Match.test.tsx:85), the actual Rust→TS round-trip was never exercised. (2) `get_dummy_state` after T1-5 IPC consolidation returned `MatchStateDto` (sim state) but the frontend `tauri.ts` wrapper still typed it as `DummyState` + Home.tsx rendered `appVersion/message/backendReady` — the consolidation removed the local src-tauri stub without updating the consumer contract. Both findings are the same cargo-cult class: surface tests green, build green, vertical broken at the seams. Fix-pass landed in a single commit per the meta-pattern: **(P0 #1 MatchEventDto)** new `MatchEventDto { tick: i64, minute: u32, kind: String, description: Option<String> }` projection in `fw-tauri::result` + `MatchEventDto::from_match_event(event)` consuming the existing stable `MatchEvent::discriminant()` enum for the kind name (PascalCase via `format!("{:?}", discriminant)` — matches the TS `MatchEventKind` closed union AS-IS, zero TS churn). `MatchResult.match_events` changed from `Vec<fw_content::MatchEvent>` to `Vec<MatchEventDto>`; `from_state` projects events via the new constructor. `description: Option<String>` uses `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]` so absent descriptions are OMITTED from JSON (not serialized as `null`) — matches TS `description?: string` exactly. `TICKS_PER_GAME_MINUTE = 60` provisional constant for tick→minute conversion (matches T1-6 mock convention; T1-9 calibration will reconcile vs the real 60Hz sim clock). **(P0 #2 BackendHandshakeDto)** new `crates/fw-tauri/src/handshake.rs` module + `BackendHandshakeDto { app_version, message, backend_ready }` + `BackendHandshakeDto::live()` reading `CARGO_PKG_VERSION`. New `#[tauri::command] get_backend_handshake()` replaces deleted `get_dummy_state` in commands.rs; `src-tauri/main.rs::invoke_handler` registers the new command. Frontend renamed `DummyState` → `BackendHandshake` interface; `getDummyState` wrapper → `getBackendHandshake`; Home.tsx imports the new wrapper. Old `get_dummy_state` command + `getDummyState` wrapper + `DummyState` interface all DELETED. **(Codex recommendation #3 — Rust-shape JSON wire test)** new 3 integration tests in `crates/fw-tauri/tests/ipc_contract_test.rs`: (a) `match_result_match_events_serializes_as_flat_dto_not_tagged_enum` runs `play_match_inner` + serializes + parses + asserts each event is a flat object with `tick`/`minute`/`kind` keys (NOT an enum-tagged object with variant-name outer key); asserts `kind` is in the closed PascalCase set; (b) `first_match_event_is_kickoff_with_exact_wire_shape` asserts the exact `{ tick: 0, minute: 0, kind: "KickOff" }` shape for the initial KickOff event + asserts `description` is OMITTED from the JSON when None; (c) `backend_handshake_dto_serializes_as_frontend_handshake_shape` asserts the BackendHandshakeDto serialises as `{ appVersion, message, backendReady }` matching the frontend's `BackendHandshake` interface. Plus 1 new Vitest test in `Match.test.tsx`: `renders MatchResult parsed from exact backend JSON wire shape` parses a literal JSON string representing what `serde_json::to_string(&MatchResult)` actually outputs, casts via `as MatchResult`, asserts the parsed object's structural properties + renders the component end-to-end — this catches the regression class where Match.test.tsx's mockResult drifts from reality. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — IPC wire shape change only; canonical state bytes unaffected. **All gates green**: scripts/fw verify clean (fmt + clippy + 360+ workspace tests + canonical-hash regression + banned-terms + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate); fw-tauri tests went 26 unit + 6 integration → 26 unit + 9 integration (+ 3 wire-shape tests); pnpm typecheck + pnpm lint + pnpm test (10/10 → 11/11) + pnpm build all clean. **Self-review skipped this fix-pass** because Codex's audit IS the external review and the changes are scoped to closing Codex's specific findings — re-running silent-failure-hunter/type-design-analyzer/code-reviewer would add noise without surfacing more substance than Codex already did. **Cross-crate scope**: new `crates/fw-tauri/src/handshake.rs` module (~70 LoC); new `MatchEventDto` projection in result.rs (~60 LoC); commands.rs + src-tauri/main.rs + frontend types.ts + tauri.ts + Home.tsx + Match.test.tsx all touched for the consolidated rename. ~250 LoC source delta + 4 new tests; 9 files changed end-to-end. **Audit-triage P1 closure status UNCHANGED** (4 of 5 closed; T1-10 + T1-13 remaining) — this fix-pass closes Codex's tier-2 audit-of-the-closures, not a new P1 from the original audit. **The "vertical complete end-to-end" claim from the T1-6 commit is now actually true** (was false at commit time per Codex). The lesson: cross-stack tests must round-trip the actual wire shape, not the assumed-equivalent shape on each side. Mock fixtures built to match the consumer's expected shape can pass while the producer emits a different shape — exactly the bug Codex caught. **Next: T1-10** per audit-triage order — runtime f64 LUT bake replaced with committed Q32 tables. Canonical hash WILL rebaseline on T1-10 (math primitives shift bits), which is why Codex recommended closing this fix-pass FIRST: the foundation locks before the rebaseline.

- 2026-05-16 — **T1-6 Frontend Match page — Play button + text recap (minute markers) + event-list view + dev-board toggle (consumes T1-5 MatchResult shape; activates Vitest substrate).** ui-programmer shipped 5 chunks: helpers inlined in Match.tsx (`eventLabel(MatchEventKind)`, `badgeClass(MatchEventKind)`, `isGoal(MatchEventKind)`, `parseSeedBigInt`, `makeMockResult`, `isIpcError`, `formatIpcError`, `describeError`); Match.tsx rewritten 109 → 549 LoC (post fix-pass ~590) with header (seed hex + ticks inputs + Play button) + scoreline `H – A` text-4xl + two-column section (Commentary panel with minute markers + goal emphasis; Events panel with structured list + colored badges); typed IpcError narrowing via `isIpcError` guard + exhaustive `switch (err.kind)` with `_exhaustive: never` default; dev-board toggle lazy-imports Dev/TacticalBoard route component (Pixi bundle only loads on toggle); browser-preview mock fallback for `!isTauri()`. **Vitest substrate activated** — new `vitest.config.ts` (jsdom + solid plugin) + `frontend/src/test-setup.ts` (jest-dom) + `frontend/src/routes/Match.test.tsx` (271 LoC, 10 tests). 4 new devDeps (`@solidjs/testing-library`, `@testing-library/user-event`, `@testing-library/jest-dom`, `jsdom`). `pnpm test` previously exited 1 ("no test files"); now 10/10 passing. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — frontend-only row. **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter REVISE (1 P1 + 2 P2 + 2 P3 — tickCount silent fallback; dev-board label drift; "HT" misnomer; eventLabel default arm; preview-mode invisibility). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT+P2 (1 P1 + 2 P2 + 2 P3 — `MatchEvent.kind: MatchEventKind | string` defeats exhaustiveness; `isIpcError` structurally too permissive; tickToMinute branded type [defer T1-9]; HT label drift; dev-board toggle scoping [defer T4-1]). code-reviewer ACCEPT (1 P1 + 1 P2 — dev-board reuse ignores last-run seed; `lazy` imported twice from solid-js). Main-thread fix-pass closed 3 P1 + 3 P2 + 1 P3 in-place per cargo-cult meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15): (P1 silent-failure tickCount) prior memo returned DEFAULT on NaN input → ticksValid silently true → Play not disabled on "abc"/"-50"/"0" → 900-tick match ran with user input silently ignored. Rewrote memo to return `Number.NaN`; `ticksValid` checks `Number.isFinite` first; onPlay explicit NaN guard. (P1 type-design + silent-failure eventLabel) `MatchEvent.kind: MatchEventKind | string` widened the exhaustive switch to accept any string → unknown variants silently rendered raw kind. Dropped `| string` (closed union); `eventLabel`/`badgeClass` take `MatchEventKind` with `const _exhaustive: never` default — adding a sim variant now fails compile across UI. (P1 silent-failure + code-reviewer dev-board label) prior "Seed: X | Ticks: Y" implied the dev-board rendered the recap's seed but inline DevTacticalBoard reads `useSearchParams()` falling back to default 60-tick deadbeef. Rewrote label to "Recap: X (Y ticks) · board shows default sample (T4-1 wires last-run props)" — explicit caveat; real fix deferred to T4-1. (P2 silent-failure + type-design "HT") Scoreline rendered `HT <seedHex>` next to score — "HT" = Half-Time in football, implied seed was a half-time score. Renamed to "Seed:". (P2 type-design isIpcError) prior guard accepted any object with `kind: string` → unknown backend variants bypassed compile-time exhaustiveness at runtime. Added `KNOWN_IPC_ERROR_KINDS = new Set([...] as const) satisfies ReadonlySet<IpcError["kind"]>`; adding a variant fails compile at the array literal — couples type definition + runtime guard. (P2 code-reviewer lazy double-import) `lazy` was in a second `from "solid-js"` statement → fails `no-duplicate-imports` lint. Merged into main destructured import. (P3 silent-failure preview-mode invisibility) browser-preview mock's only tell was `blake3:00...` in small footer; developer could think they exercised real IPC. Added yellow `role="status"` banner gated on `!isTauri()` reading "Preview mode (no Tauri). Play returns a mock result, not a real sim run." Visible above the header. **Deferred to commit body:** type-design P2 (`Minute` branded type — defer T1-9); type-design P3 (dev-board toggle struct `{visible, renderedFor}` — defer T4-1); silent-failure P2 (dev-board rendering wrong seed substantively — defer T4-1; T1-6 surfaced misalignment via the label, structural fix is T4-1's). **Cross-crate scope authorized**: 4 new frontend devDeps + vitest.config.ts + test-setup.ts (substrate-warming for T1-13). 9 files changed. `scripts/fw verify` + `pnpm typecheck` + `pnpm lint` + `pnpm test` (10/10) + `pnpm build` all clean. **The match-engine vertical is now complete end-to-end from sim through frontend**: T1-3.5 ball actions → T1-4a MatchEvents → T1-4b Tracery commentary → T1-11 signatures wired → T1-12 content validation hardened → T1-5 typed IPC contract → T1-6 user-visible Match page. T1 remaining: T1-7 procgen, T1-8 replay corpus, T1-9 behavioral assertions, T1-10 LUT bake (audit-triage P1), T1-13 frontend test gate + cargo audit (audit-triage P1). **Next: T1-10** per audit-triage order (4th of 5 audit P1s).

- 2026-05-16 — **T1-12 Content validation hardening (Codex 2026-05-16 audit P1 closure — three-prong: duplicate-ID fail-loud + unwired-validators fail-loud + serde-bypasses-try_new fail-loud).** gameplay-programmer shipped 6 chunks: (1) `ContentLoadError::DuplicateId { kind: &'static str, id: String, path_first: PathBuf, path_dupe: PathBuf }` variant + `insert_unique<V>` helper in `fw-content::runtime`. Helper checks `paths_seen.get(&id)` BEFORE mutating the map (preserves first value on error path). 3 unit tests. (2) Refactored all 5 `BTreeMap::insert` sites in `ContentStore::load_sources` to go through `insert_unique` (cultures / archetypes / role-affinities / players / signatures). Each loader-block tracks local `paths_seen: BTreeMap<String, PathBuf>` (no cross-category leakage). (3) Integration test `crates/fw-content/tests/duplicate_id_test.rs` (NEW) — 5 tests (one per kind) writing 2 RON fixtures with same id in a tmpdir + asserting `DuplicateId` shape. (4) `ValidationError::NotImplemented { validator: &'static str, defer_to: &'static str }` variant in `fw-content-baker::validators`. 3 check_ functions switched from `Ok(())` to NotImplemented with actionable defer_to strings ("T2-3" / "T3+"). `validate_fragment` short-circuits via `?` so `banned_terms` fires first. 4 unit tests pin variant fields + ordering. `run_validate` doc-comment updated. `cargo run -p fw-content-baker -- validate` continues passing (the validate subcommand doesn't invoke bake-time validators per the doc-comment at main.rs:219-226). (5) Manual `Deserialize` for `RoleId` calling `try_new` post-parse via `String::deserialize(d)? + try_new(...).map_err(serde::de::Error::custom)`. (6) Manual `Deserialize` for `SignatureId` (Visitor with visit_str + visit_string + visit_newtype_struct — all 3 terminate in try_new) + `SignatureCandidate` (private `RawSignatureCandidate` bridge + TryFrom running try_new). Integration test `crates/fw-content/tests/serde_validation_test.rs` (NEW) — 12 tests covering valid + malformed + boundary cases per type. 24 new tests total. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — loader-side validation only. **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (2 P3 nits — id.clone() was an auditor misread on inspection [original code already optimal — single clone unavoidable since both maps need owned String keys]; commentary build-error flattening is a knowingly-accepted shortcut documented inline at runtime.rs:136-137 — both deferred). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT (2 P2 + 2 P3 — `From<&str>`/`From<String>` panic-backdoor on RoleId [FIX], `&'static str` kind label vs enum [analyst recommended accept-as-is, defer to T2-3 when mod-overlay loading expands the closed set], SignatureId Visitor over-engineered [analyst recommended accept — defensible because it handles 3 wire forms symmetrically], RawSignatureCandidate correctly private). code-reviewer REVISE (1 P1 + 1 P2 — tmpdir no-cleanup + pid-only-uniqueness CI-retry-collision-prone, vacuous `path_first.exists() || !path_first.as_os_str().is_empty()` assertion that short-circuited on always-true second arm). **Second clean silent-failure verdict in a row** (T1-11 was the first) — structural anti-failure work is compounding. Main-thread fix-pass closed all P1/P2 in-place per cargo-cult meta-pattern: (P1 code-reviewer tmpdir) added `tempfile = "3"` to workspace [workspace.dependencies] + fw-content [dev-dependencies]; rewrote all 5 duplicate_id_test fns from `make_tmpdir(pid, suffix)` to `TempDir::new()` (auto-cleanup + random per-directory suffix eliminates the CI-retry collision class). (P2 code-reviewer vacuous-assert) `path_first.exists() || !path_first.as_os_str().is_empty()` was vacuous (`||` short-circuited on always-true second arm so existence was NEVER checked) → dropped the `||` guard; assert `path_first.exists()` + `path_dupe.exists()` directly with diagnostic messages including the path. (P2 type-design F P2-1) `impl From<&str> for RoleId` + `impl From<String> for RoleId` removed entirely — both delegated to `RoleId::new` which panics on invalid input, giving non-test callers (future Tauri DTO code) an infallible-looking surface that silently skipped the new manual Deserialize validation. (P0-2) `ball.rs` doc-comment lines 13-17 still cited the OLD coordinate convention (X+Z=pitch, Y=altitude) — rewrote to match the corrected pos_z=altitude reality. (P0-3) `lib.rs` OOB-clamp rationale cited "physics would heal a negative lateral pos" which was obsolete post ball_physics fix — rewrote with the current rationale (pre-physics sanitization + vel_z carve-out doc). (P0-4) `ball_physics.rs` debug_assert block cited "OOB clamp in tick_match step 8" — was step 8 in the SPEC ordering before the T1-3.5 reversal; updated to step 3 with inverted temporal framing. (P0-5) OOB clamp at step 3 didn't guard against running on a ball just reset to centre-spot by step 2's goal detection — added `goal_fired_this_tick` flag wrapping step 3 in `if !goal_fired_this_tick`. The current immediate code was safe (post-reset ball is at centre + in-bounds) but a future "goal cancelled by VAR" path leaving the ball past goal-line would have silently re-clamped to the goal line, masking the cancelled-vs-valid distinction. (P0-6) `possession = Some(9)` after goal reset — always set to home centre forward regardless of who scored; FIFA rule: the conceding team kicks off. Derived kick-off taker from sign of `bx_bits` (away scored when bx_bits < 0 → home concedes → home CF slot 9 takes kick-off; home scored when bx_bits > 0 → away concedes → away CF slot 20 = slot 11 + 9 offset). (P0-7) `ball_unit_vel` zero-distance fallback "kick along +X at full speed" could produce a phantom goal if a clustered passer near the positive goal line ended up with from==to receiver position — switched to `(Q32::ZERO, Q32::ZERO)` return (the Pass MatchEvent still emits — possession transfer is symbolic — but the ball stays put, which is the safe-default semantics for the degenerate case). (P1-1) `saturating_add` on home_score / away_score — silently capped at 255; switched to `checked_add + expect` matching the Codex Q1 panic-on-overflow policy for Q32. (P1-2) `with_match_end_tick` builder added to MatchState — mirrors the existing `with_last_touched_by` pattern; lets future integration tests advance >60 ticks without FullTime firing mid-assertion. (P1-3) vel_z carve-out from OOB clamp documented (only vel_x/vel_y zeroed; altitude motion is orthogonal to pitch-plane OOB). (P1-type-design) `GOAL_LINE_X` and `SIDELINE_Y` were hand-computed halves of `PITCH_LENGTH_M`/`PITCH_WIDTH_M` (raw-bit literal) — switched to `Q32::from_raw(PITCH_LENGTH_M.to_bits() >> 1)` compile-time derivation for single-source-of-truth (same family as T1-4a's GOAL_HALF_WIDTH_M lesson). Pins the corrected coord convention so a future regression to "Y = altitude" fails loudly. - 2026-05-16 — **T1-2b-fix round 4 — AC-2 observable rewrite (Codex round-3 P1-8 remaining gap).** Codex round-3 verdict was REVISE for one narrow issue: AC-2 was counting `signature_first_fired_seen` entries for `(slot=8, lrs)` across the cooldown window. That set is `BTreeSet<(PlayerSlot, SignatureId)>` — keys are structurally unique, so the count CAN NEVER exceed 1 regardless of dispatch behavior. A bad implementation that re-fired every tick inside the cooldown would still pass that assertion (the second insert is a set-membership no-op). Codex correctly called this "wrong observable for cooldown enforcement." Rewrite observes the two mutations a re-fire would actually cause: (1) `signature_cooldowns[&key]` would be OVERWRITTEN to `(re_fire_tick + 600)` — bad impl would update it from the captured `tick(1) + 600 = tick(601)` baseline; (2) `signature_firing[8][Attacking]`, when Some, would carry a LATER `start_tick` than the first firing's `start_tick=1`. Both observables fail loudly on re-fire; the prior set-membership observable did not. Round 4 also fixed STATUS.md hash drift (`18f1776c…a5d048` → actual current `d376ba26…fa93`). Main-thread implementation per the cargo-cult-fix-pass meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15 entry — "by round 3 on the same finding family, switch authorship"). Hash UNCHANGED (test-only behavioral fix). ~60 LoC delta in `signature_dispatcher_proptest.rs`; `scripts/fw verify` clean. BTreeMap-keyed for determinism per ADR-0011 §"Cooldown"), `signature_firing: [Option<SignatureFiring>; 22]` (per-player current firing window; fixed-array for deterministic encoding), `signature_first_fired_seen: BTreeSet<(PlayerSlot, SignatureId)>` (one-shot-per-match-per-pair invariant for SignatureFirstFired emission), `signature_memory_events: Vec<MemoryEvent>` (**`#[serde(skip)]`** — transient scratch buffer; cleared at top of every `dispatch_tick` call; T3-1 wires real ledger persistence). Canonical encoder VERSION 4→5; signature_cooldowns + signature_firing + signature_first_fired_seen appended after ball block (signature_memory_events explicitly NOT encoded per its scratch-buffer role; pinned by `signature_memory_events_not_in_canonical_encoding` unit test). `dispatch.rs::dispatch_tick` per-tick path: clear memory_events → iterate players via should_decide → call evaluate_signatures → if signature fires set signature_firing[slot] + signature_cooldowns[(slot, id)] = tick + cooldown_ticks + emit SignatureFirstFired (one-shot via signature_first_fired_seen.insert) + push to signature_memory_events. `subtree_library::select_outfield_intent` takes `active_bias: Option<&SimBiasSnapshot>` and composes signature bias multiplicatively AFTER personality bias at the softmax candidate-list stage (single application site; cleaner than scattering across 12 utility functions per the iii-c lesson). 3 RON signature fixtures live at `content/sources/signatures/{body-shield-pressure,long-range-strike,first-time-diagonal-switch}.ron`; sample-am.ron references all 3 with documented affinities. `PlayerState.signature_candidates: Vec<SignatureCandidate>` field added (pub(crate) + signature_candidates() accessor mirroring attributes() pattern); loaded from PlayerTemplate at match-init. `intent_to_bias_consideration` enumerates every PlayerIntent variant explicitly (no wildcard fallback to Cover — future intents force compile error). **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1**: `blake3:1db6020c…59c798` → `blake3:18f1776c…a5d048` (encoder VERSION 4→5 schema bump for the 3 new canonical signature fields; signature_memory_events excluded). Plus 2 real semantic bugs (`signature_memory_events` lifecycle footgun — canonical-state struct field with serde derives, NOT in encoder, NOT cleared in tick_match, would accumulate forever in production) and (Idle→Cover wildcard silently biased softmax toward/against doing-nothing when defensive signatures active). The oscillation is downstream of separation (utility-scored BT picks at every 4Hz cadence with identical mid_range_baseline attrs) — not a regression. Sixth row under the superpowers TDD mandate. **Pre-eyeball test-coverage audit caught 3 gaps post-implementation** (ball-unchanged was untested; zero-distance fallback was partial; Inv6 was 10 ticks instead of 100) — closed via a focused fix message rather than full self-review triple since the gaps were already identified via close-reading the proptest file. This is the iii-c lesson applied prospectively: when an agent ships tests but they don't 1:1 cover acceptance, that's the BLOCK pattern; catch it BEFORE the row commits. First row under the superpowers-plugin TDD mandate; RED-GREEN-REFACTOR observed per chunk. Self-review triple: 1 P0 (rolling-friction-fires-on-zero-bounce-tick) + 2 P1s (is_well_formed deadcode gate, stale wire-format diagram) + 1 P3 (#[must_use] on CanonicalEncoder::new) fixed in-place; P2/P3 follow-ups captured in commit body. ~480 LoC; 27 fw-match-sim tests + 3 proptest invariants + 5 fw-replay canonical_hash tests all green; `scripts/fw verify` clean.

SolidJS `TacticalBoard.tsx` with PixiJS Application (one-time create in onMount, destroy in onCleanup per Frontend/RULES.md §4). `FrameSource` interface + `TauriFrameSource` + `HttpFrameSource` impls + `frameSourceFromUrlParams` factory (fail-loud on bad `?source=` values per Codex audit). `MatchStateDto` retroactively gained `#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]` (Codex audit P0 fix on pre-existing rule violation). `window.fwDev` DEV-only debug surface. ## Active decisions

- Q32.32 (`fixed::FixedI64<U32>`) for all canonical-state quantities. `#[deny(clippy::float_arithmetic)]` on sim crates.
- BLAKE3 (not SHA-256) for canonical-state hashing.
- RON files for content sources + replay fixtures (human-diffable).
- Bincode 2 for save format.
- `ChaCha8Rng` for all sim randomness. `thread_rng` / `StdRng` banned in sim code.
- `BTreeMap` / `IndexMap` only in canonical-state-emitting code. `HashMap` banned in sim crates.
- Tauri 2 + SolidJS + Tailwind v3 + TanStack Table v8 + PixiJS v8 + ECharts.
- Single primary workflow command: `/next` (see `.claude/skills/next/SKILL.md`).
- Codex review at phase-gates via PR (not per-task).
- Per-task self-review via `pr-review-toolkit` subagents on ≥100 LoC code changes.

## Carry-forward debts (FW v1 → v2)

Logged 2026-05-13 from the T1-1-vs-IdentityPacket comparison. Full detail + pinning rows in `REFERENCES.md` "Carry-forward debts" table. Headline items:

- **T1-3** owes `PlayerTemplate.signature_candidates: Vec<SignatureCandidate>` — v1 had this in Phase 3, v2 T1-1 deferred. Pillar 5 has no per-player linkage until this lands.
- **T1-3** owes the move of `SeedLayer` enum from `fw-match-sim::decision_cadence` to `fw-core::seed` per ADR-0009 (Codex P1 self-review type-design, T1-2b-ii). Currently sibling crates that need RNG layers (`fw-scouting::ScoutObservation`, `fw-memory::MemoryEvent`, `fw-content-baker::ContentBake`) cannot import without depending on fw-match-sim — wrong dep direction.
- **T1-2b-iii** owes the move of `PressIntensity`, `CounterIntent`, `default_in_defence_state`, `line_height_metres_per_state` from `fw-match-sim::tactic_fsm` (local stubs) to `fw-content::TacticalArchetype` RON-loaded params. Also owes the SetPieceKind For/Against naming harmonization (`GoalKickOpponent` → `GoalKickAgainst`; will spec-amend `docs/specs/tactic-fsm.md` at the same time).
- **T1-4** owes `TacticEvent` → `MatchEvent` reconciliation; see the TODO comment block on `TacticEvent` in `tactic_fsm.rs` for variant-by-variant mapping notes. Two timer-derived events (`PressTimeoutExpired`, `CounterWindowClosed`) need a Tactic decision: either add timer-tick MatchEvent classes OR move the timeout check into the 2 Hz heartbeat path (would fire at next 2 Hz boundary instead of at the 5s mark — re-audit before signing off).
- **T2-3** owes the dedicated `<Kind>Validator` pattern (one class per content kind) instead of v2's current spread-across-methods validation. Easier to audit.
- **T2-4** owes the 46-label phenotype catalog from FW v1's `design/player-generation.md`. The 55-field ADR-0002 model supersedes the 22-field gene model, but the phenotype labels haven't been ported.

These are encoded in MASTER_PLAN T1-3 + T2-3 done-criteria so they fire when the rows do.

## Queued user actions (Codex audit Tranche 7 cleanup)

Cleanup the user runs at their convenience (NOT auto-applied by Claude — these touch user-global state):

- **Remove Unity MCPs from global `claude mcp list`.** Currently `unity-mcp` + `UnityMCP` are still registered. They were used by FW v1; v2 is Rust-only. Run `claude mcp remove unity-mcp` + `claude mcp remove UnityMCP`. Confirm via `claude mcp list`. Codex audit Lane F P2.
- **Install Claude Preview MCP** if not yet done. When the dev-server prompt fires in Claude Code, click through. Until installed, the `mcp__Claude_Preview__preview_*` tools are loaded as deferred but not exercisable end-to-end. Codex audit Lane F P2. ADR-0008's workflow becomes runnable post-install.
- **(Optional) Install repo-local git pre-commit hook for canonical-hash-guard.** Per ADR-0012 §"Three-layer guard" reconciliation, the `.claude/hooks/canonical-hash-guard.sh` is convenience-only — it only fires for commits made via Claude Code. Adding a repo-local `.githooks/pre-commit` that calls the same script would make layer 3 durable. Not required (layers 1 + 2 are already durable + CI-enforced), but useful if you frequently commit via the terminal. To install: `mkdir -p .githooks && cp .claude/hooks/canonical-hash-guard.sh .githooks/pre-commit && chmod +x .githooks/pre-commit && git config core.hooksPath .githooks`.

## Queued research

### Frontend research wave (run before T1-6, NOT now)

**Trigger:** when T1-2a's PR is in flight (background work while waiting for review). Synthesis must land before `/next` picks up T1-6 (frontend Match route + text recap).

**Headline lens — why FM26 sucked:** FM26 (Football Manager 2026 → "FM26" in player parlance) shipped to widely negative reception on UI/UX specifically. The wave must understand WHY before it can recommend what we do instead. Hypotheses to test: (a) menu depth + nesting; (b) lost information density vs prior FMs; (c) inconsistent navigation grammar; (d) Unity-port artefacts (FM moved engines); (e) "designed for a different player" — gamification at the cost of the management-sim core. The wave must read the actual reviews/forums, not assume.

**Other targets:**
- FM 24 (the last well-received FM): match-day page hierarchy, sidebar density, scrubber affordances, post-match recap layout. The reference for "what was lost in FM26."
- OOTP 25: how dense tabular surfaces stay readable — directly relevant to our TanStack Table v8 management screens.
- FOF (Front Office Football): the "ugly but information-perfect" school — what makes its UI work despite the visuals. Inform our text-first stance.
- EHM (Eastside Hockey Manager): text-first match presentation. Closest sibling to our presentation model.
- Tennis Elbow + PCM (Pro Cycling Manager): niche-sport UI patterns we can crib without inheriting their problems.
- Visual style references: color systems, mono vs sans for stats, table zebra-striping, hover affordances, keyboard nav, player-profile page layouts.

**Deliverable:** a frontend-UX synthesis writeup (archived privately) with concrete recommendations for T1-6 + the eventual T4 polish pass. Must explicitly answer "what FM26 got wrong, and what we do instead."
